Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The trees operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 249. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 403 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the trees space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 249 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 403. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the trees framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 27. The resulting operator achieves bound O(459/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by trees generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 459. QED.
